- **forgemaster-mini**: Run the **low-ceremony** Forge path for small, clear-scope work (bugs, chores, config, single-file changes). - **ft.dictdump**: Dumps all terms in the given dictionary ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.dictdump.md) · ★80)

- **ft.explain**: Returns the execution plan for a complex query ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.explain.md) · ★80)
- **ft.explaincli**: Returns the execution plan for a complex query ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.explaincli.md) · ★80)

- **ft.info**: Returns information and statistics on the index ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.info.md) · ★80)

- **ft.sugdel**: Deletes a string from a suggestion index ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.sugdel.md) · ★80)

- **ft.suglen**: Gets the size of an auto-complete suggestion dictionary ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.suglen.md) · ★80)

- **ft.tagvals**: Returns the distinct tags indexed in a Tag field ([redis/docs](https://github.com/redis/docs/blob/HEAD/content/commands/ft.tagvals.md) · ★80)
